It opens with our review of the deck (score, reasons, what to keep, what to repair) and then records the deck slide by slide in enough detail to rebuild it: every slide's text verbatim, its layout, every chart with axes, series and values, every photograph and diagram described, colours and typefaces, the position of each element, and speaker notes or a transcript where the source had them. ## Review

**5 out of 10, Needs substantial work.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a coherent navy and orange identity, strong full-bleed photography and clear section dividers, so the route from booking platform to festival to team is easy to follow. But much of the information sits in hairline, low-contrast grey type that is barely legible even at readable size (slides 3, 4, 10, 36 to 40), and the contact sheets show the same problem on the problem, vision and overview slides. The concept and mood come through, yet the business case, such as the revenue model, the raise and the financials, is thin or missing from the deck itself: slide 32 only says 'Please see appendix' and slide 33 is a single line. Text on imagery (slide 12 over a busy collage, slide 14 over the figure) fights the background, and the influencer pages (21 to 24) are tiny repeated grids that cannot be read.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the navy and orange palette, the one-word divider slides and the full-bleed atmospheric photography on slides 13, 14 and 41.

**Most useful repair:** Raise body text size and weight and contrast on slides 3, 4, 10 and 36 to 40, and give the raise, financials and use of funds real content instead of one-line slides 32 and 33.

**What would change the score:** I would cut the four influencer grids to a few featured names with a total reach figure, and place the headline text on slide 12 over a plain darkened panel.

_Confidence high. 43 of 43 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-09. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck is a private placement memorandum for Fyre, a talent booking marketplace that also planned a music festival on a private Exuma island. It opens with a logo cover, a confidentiality notice and a legal disclaimer, then a brand statement. The middle sections cover the booking problem and Fyre's marketplace, its 10% buyer fee and talent benefit model, then the festival concept, influencer marketing campaign, press coverage, brand partnerships, land deal and a $25mm raise. A team section introduces the founders and staff with photographs and short bios, and the deck closes with quotations and the logo. Several slides are built around large lists of influencers and their follower counts, while a few use full-bleed photography with a single headline. Slides worth seeing include the revenue model slide, the cover-to-section rhythm, and the festival quote slide over imagery. The layout uses a consistent navy and orange palette with generous spacing, though many slides carry dense legal or lists text.

## Questions about this deck

**What does the Fyre deck say about Fyre's revenue model and buyer fee?**

Slide 10 says Fyre assesses a 10% fee to buyers and does not take a commission from talent on bookings. It also redirects 25% of the Fyre fee (2.5% of the booking) to talent by way of benefits.

**What land deal does the Fyre presentation describe?**

Slide 31 says Fyre has been given $8.4mm of market value land on Black Point, Exuma in exchange for hosting the festival and advertising the island.

**How much is the Fyre raise and what will it fund according to the deck?**

Slide 33 states a $25mm raise to acquire 500 exclusive managers and expand Fyre globally.

**How many pages does the Fyre deck have and how does it begin?**

The Fyre deck has 43 pages. Slide 1 is the logo cover and slide 2 is the confidentiality notice for the private placement memorandum.

**Text**

THE PROBLEM
RECORDED MUSIC REVENUE IS UNDER PRESSURE.
ARTISTS ARE SEEKING NEW WAYS
OF EARNING REVENUE AS THE
MUSIC INDUSTRY BUSINESS MODEL
HAS BECOME DISRUPTED.
LIVE BOOKINGS IS THE FASTEST GROWING
SEGMENT OF THE ENTERTAINMENT INDUSTRY
with rising ticket prices and increasing
attendance. Fans seek more meaningful
connections to talent through live events
and social. Talent seek to increase their
engagement with fans.
BUT, WE’VE SEEN FIRSTHAND
THAT THE LIVE INDUSTRY IS BROKEN.
Accessing talent is a mystifying, inefficient,
and inconsistent process: there is no platform
that allows buyers to connect with talent
through one platform, submitting offers,
negotiating and processing
payment, end-to-end.

### Slide 7

**Text**

FYRE
FYRE REMOVES THE FRICTION TO SECURING TALENT
Fyre is a global entertainment marketplace that helps / venues, brands, and qualified private buyers book talent / for live performances, appearances, and paid social / posts through one consistent and easy digital platform.
Since launching in May 2016, thousands of / offers representing tens of millions of dollars of / performances and appearances have been made / and accepted with Fyre.
MUSICIANS
ATHLETES
MODELS
INFLUENCERS

**Layout**

Two-column split: the left half is a white panel with a centred text stack (wordmark, bold tagline, two centred body paragraphs) and a phone mockup centred below it, about 9 to 40 percent of the width. The right half is a full-bleed 2x2 grid of four photo tiles, each 25 percent wide and 50 percent tall, with the category label centred in each tile. There is no visible footer, slide number or source line.

**Visuals**

Four full-bleed photographs in a 2x2 grid on the right half: top-left Musicians (drummer with cymbals in monochrome), top-right Athletes (basketball player dunking under a Stanley Cup Champions banner, in colour with a dark arena), bottom-left Models (monochrome close-up of a woman with long hair facing the camera), bottom-right Influencers (man in a dark cap and orange patterned shirt with hands clasped, with a grey tint). One photographic phone mockup: a white smartphone with a gold-white bezel shown front-on, its screen displaying the app header TALENT, a FYRE EXCLUSIVES card with a black-and-white photo of a man with dreadlocks in a star-print tank top captioned WAKA FLOCKA, and a FYRE TALENT / CATEGORY: ALL filter bar with a partial photo of blonde hair below.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF on the left half, with a dark navy #001B2B rectangle behind the photo grid on the right half. White background #FFFFFF on the left, with a dark navy #001B2B fill behind the right photo grid. Wordmark and tagline in black #000000 DemiBold sans-serif (geometric, like Visby), with wide letter-spacing. Body text is black #000000 Thin geometric sans-serif, sentence case, light weight, with wide letter-spacing, roughly 1.6 percent of slide height. Category labels are white #FFFFFF DemiBold geometric sans-serif in capitals with slight letter-spacing at 3.65 percent of slide height. Photo tiles are hard-edged rectangles with no borders, corner radii, shadows or gradients; tiles are monochrome or desaturated except the athletes tile, and the influencers tile has a light grey overlay.

TALENT NETWORK
JAMIE FOXX / QUEEN LATIFAH / IGGY AZALEA / LIL WAYNE / DJ KHALED / JESSE JO STARK
FYRE’S ROSTER INCLUDES SOME OF THE MOST ICONIC NAMES IN ENTERTAINMENT INCLUDING:
LIL WAYNE, DJ KHALED, ANTONIO PIERCE, JAMIE FOXX,
QUEEN LATIFAH, AND HUNDREDS OF OTHER NOTABLE ARTISTS, ATHLETES AND INFLUENCERS.

**Layout**

Centred single-column composition on a white field. The title sits centred near the top at about 10% from the top edge. A full-width row of six portrait photographs runs edge to edge (no margins, no gaps) from about 30% to 64% of the slide height, with one centred caption under each photo. A thin centred intro line sits at about 74.5% and a centred two-line roster statement sits at about 80.7% to 86.5%. There is no footer, page number or logo.

**Visuals**

Six portrait photographs in a single horizontal row, each cropped to the same height and placed edge to edge. Left to right: (1) Jamie Foxx: a man in a black tuxedo with bow tie holding a brown bottle in his left hand and a snifter of amber liquor in his right, standing against a pale grey-blue studio backdrop, full-length framing cropped at the knees, soft even studio lighting, dominant colours black, grey-blue and amber. (2) Queen Latifah: black-and-white close-up of a woman laughing with her mouth open and teeth showing, hair blown across the left side of her face, white collared shirt, grey backdrop, soft front lighting, monochrome. (3) Iggy Azalea: blonde wavy hair, right hand touching the side of her head, gold ring, dark plum lipstick, dark navy off-shoulder top, warm light background, shoulders cropped at the bottom edge, dominant colours gold, navy and plum. (4) Lil Wayne: black-and-white tight head-and-shoulders portrait, face tattoos, cap, direct gaze, white collar, neutral grey background, hard lighting, monochrome. (5) DJ Khaled: head-and-shoulders portrait on a flat pale pink background, bearded man with gold grillz and gold chain held in his teeth, raised fist, black leather jacket, gold watch, dominant colours pink, black and gold. (6) Jesse Jo Stark: black-and-white three-quarter portrait of a woman with long dark hair, black top, looking at camera, a shelf with objects at the upper right, light background, cropped at the right edge of the slide. All photos are rectangular, unboxed, with no overlay or tint, and sit flush with each other. No logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Minimal monochrome look on a white field. Title and names in Visby CF DemiBold, black #000000, all capitals with wide letter-spacing, title about 3.65% of slide height. Captions in Visby CF DemiBold, black #000000, all capitals, about 1.46% of slide height. Intro line in Visby CF Thin, black #000000, all capitals, slightly tracked, about 1.58% of slide height. Roster statement in Visby CF DemiBold, black #000000, all capitals, tracked, about 2.43% of slide height, two centred lines. Sans-serif geometric typefaces throughout. Photos have square corners, no shadows, no borders and no gradients.

### Slide 9

**Text**

FOR BUYERS & TALENT
BUYERS
Quickly submit and negotiate
offers, and finalize payments.
TALENT
Evaluate, negotiate and
accept offers.
BUYERS & TALENT
More bookings and connections
between talent and buyers
facilitated than ever before.
WITHIN THE FYRE PLATFORM
FYRE STREAMLINES THE BOOKING PROCESS FOR
BUYERS, FACILITATING MORE TRANSACTIONS.
Fyre is the first end-to-end platform for talent bookers.
Buyers submit and negotiate offers, sign contracts and
finalize payments, all with Fyre.
We’ve built a communications and integrated payments
platform to remove pain points from the booking process
and enhance efficiency on both sides of the deal.
FYRE MAXIMIZES TALENT EARNINGS BY EXPOSING
THEIR AVAILABILITY TO QUALIFIED TALENT BUYERS
AND VENUES.
This allows for more bookings through increased visibility
to talent buyers and simplifies the day-to-day anagement.
The Fyre platform securely and confidentially manages
the entire booking process, from initial offer to payment,
and day-of coordination.

**Layout**

Centred title at the top of the slide, followed by a three-column row of headings (BUYERS, TALENT, BUYERS & TALENT) with an orange rule and arrowheads running under each heading, each column with a short description beneath. A full-width black horizontal rule sits at about 49% of the height with a centred caption below it, and below that two text columns, left starting at about 10.5% and right starting at about 55.2%, each with a bold caps lead-in and light body paragraphs. Margins are roughly 11% left and right; there is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Three small orange-outlined circle icons, one before each heading: left a black dollar sign in an orange circle outline, middle a black microphone glyph in an orange circle outline, right a black checkmark in an orange circle outline. A third orange line runs under BUYERS & TALENT without an arrowhead, ending at the right margin. A full-width black horizontal rule at about 48.8% of the height, roughly 0.3% thick, spanning about 11.3% to 89% of the width. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Titles and headings are black #000000 in Visby CF DemiBold, a geometric sans-serif, the main title and column headings set in uppercase with wide letter spacing for the title and tighter spacing for the large column headings. Body copy is black #000000 in Visby CF Thin, a light geometric sans-serif. Accent colour is orange #FF8000 used for the icon circles, the rules and the arrowheads. Main title is about 3.65% of slide height, column headings about 4.29%, column descriptions about 2.8%, and small caps captions and body text about 1.7%. Lines are straight, no corner radii, no shadows.

### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Dark red full-bleed section divider followed by a centred statement and small body copy creates strong contrast between heading and detail.
- Review: But much of the information sits in hairline, low-contrast grey type that is barely legible even at readable size (slides 3, 4, 10, 36 to 40), and the contact sheets show the same problem on the problem, vision and overview slides.
- Suggested repair: Raise body text size and weight and contrast on slides 3, 4, 10 and 36 to 40, and give the raise, financials and use of funds real content instead of one-line slides 32 and 33.

**Text**

REVENUE MODEL
FYRE ASSESSES A 10% FEE TO BUYERS AND DOES NOT
TAKE A COMMISSION FROM TALENT ON BOOKINGS
We provide incredible value to our buyers for the fee that we assess – through making the
talent booking process quicker, easier and more transparent than the previous cumbersome
model of connecting with talent. We also redirect 25% of the Fyre fee (2.5% of the booking) to talent
by way of benefits. This is unprecedented in the entertainment space and builds long-term loyalty with talent.
Our interactive real-time platform is constantly evolving to meet the needs of our talent buyers.
We work in tandem with talent management teams to maximize their talent’s revenue
and develop their fan-base.
WE TAKE NO DIRECT LIABILITY FOR ANY BOOKINGS MADE WITH FYRE. EACH AGREEMENT IS BETWEEN TALENT AND THE BUYER. IF TALENT FAILS TO PERFORM,
BUYERS ARE BACKED WITH THE FYRE GUARANTEE, WHICH REIMBURSES THEIR FEE THROUGH THIRD PARTY INSURANCE. FYRE, IN GOOD-FAITH,
WORKS WITH TALENT BUYERS TO FIND SUITABLE LAST MINUTE ALTERNATIVES.

**Layout**

Full-width photograph band across the top half (about 0 to 50.2% of slide height) with the title block centred over it, followed by a white lower half holding a centred body paragraph block and a small italic disclaimer at the bottom. Everything is centre-aligned on a single vertical axis with no visible footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

One photograph filling the top band: a blurred crowd at a concert with many raised hands silhouetted against a red-lit background. Hands are in the lower centre and right, slightly sharp in the centre-bottom, with out-of-focus red and orange light bokeh across the frame. The image is full-bleed across the full slide width, cropped to a horizontal band, with a dark overlay making the upper area nearly black maroon and the lower area red. No charts, tables, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background is white in the lower area and a dark red-black photograph in the top band. Title text is white #FFFFFF in a geometric sans-serif (Visby CF DemiBold), all capitals with wide letter-spacing; the section label REVENUE MODEL is about 3.65% of slide height and the headline about 4.26%. Body text is black #000000 in Visby CF Thin, sentence case, about 1.95% of slide height, with generous line spacing and centred alignment. Disclaimer is black #000000 in Visby CF Thin italic capitals, about 1.22% of slide height, widely letter-spaced. No accent colours, shapes, lines, radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Full-bleed photography with a large orange highlighted quote sets the festival mood effectively.
- Review: Text on imagery (slide 12 over a busy collage, slide 14 over the figure) fights the background, and the influencer pages (21 to 24) are tiny repeated grids that cannot be read.
- What would change the score: I would cut the four influencer grids to a few featured names with a total reach figure, and place the headline text on slide 12 over a plain darkened panel.

**Text**

“COME, SEEK, / FOR SEARCHING IS THE FOUNDATION / OF FORTUNE”

**Layout**

Full-bleed mosaic of seven photographs covering the whole slide, with a centred three-line quotation overlaid across the middle. The quote is set in three lines: an orange line at top, a white line in the middle, and an orange line at bottom, with curly quotation marks hanging at the line ends. The photo grid is roughly three columns by three rows, with the top-left photo spanning the full left column height and the bottom photos spanning two columns. No title placeholder, footer, slide number or margins are visible; the photos run edge to edge.

**Visuals**

Seven photographs in a mosaic, all dimmed by a dark navy tint. (1) Top-left, tall, 33.4 by 66.7 percent: aerial view of turquoise surf breaking over dark olive-green rocky reef, diagonal white foam line running from top to bottom, cropped at edges. (2) Top-centre, 33.5 by 33.5 percent: night outdoor party at dusk with silhouetted palm trees bent by wind under a dark purple-grey sky, a large purple-lit geodesic triangular frame stage in the centre, and a dense crowd silhouetted in the foreground; dominant colours violet, magenta and charcoal. (3) Top-right, 33.3 by 33.5 percent: coastal scene of a rocky green headland, pale sand beach with a thatched umbrella, turquoise shallow water and a small jet ski with a white wake; sky is pale blue-grey. A second photograph of the same beach area, 33.4 by 33.3 percent, sits directly beneath it and is not separately visible. (4) Centre, 33.5 by 33.6 percent: five young women in swimwear (red, white, and beige bikinis, one with sunglasses) seated on a white draped daybed under a white canopy with green foliage at the edges; bright, soft daylight, white and cream dominant. (5) Right, tall, 33.4 by 66.7 percent: black-and-white-toned view of a large white marquee tent ceiling with black lighting trusses and spotlights, a disco ball and hanging speaker; below, a crowd and a performer on a stage, with a microphone stand; mostly grey and white with dark tints. (6) Bottom-left, wide, 66.7 by 33.5 percent: overhead view of a woman in a black one-piece swimsuit floating face-up in clear blue pool water, arms and legs spread in a star shape, hair dark and fanned out; dominant colours deep teal-blue and skin tones. All photos are full-bleed, rectangular, not boxed, and have no rounded corners; each carries the same dark navy overlay that lowers their brightness and saturation.

**Design**

Background is a deep navy #001B2B. Quote lines use VisbyCF-DemiBold, a geometric sans-serif, in all capitals with wide letter-spacing; the orange lines are #FF7D00 and the middle line and quotation marks are #FFFFFF. The large orange words are about 7.9 percent of slide height (capital height about 5.5 percent), and the white line is about 5.3 percent of slide height. Text is centred over the mosaic with no shadow. No borders, rules or decorative shapes; the only shapes are the rectangular photo tiles, butted together with no gaps and a dark navy tint on each photo.

### Slide 15

**Text**

OUR VISION
Fyre has a unique goal and inspiration:
the exploration of the uncharted inspired by and
referencing the five elements of the earth
Throughout the next five years, we will traverse the globe to find untouched
lands and convert them into unparalleled experiences. Fyre will work to
bring life to each region.  Through the purchase of significant land, we will
utilize the each festival as a major cultural event to bring awareness, visitors
and livelihood to the land.

**Layout**

Single centred column on a two-band layout. The title OUR VISION sits centred in the white upper band, about 10% from the top of the slide. A full-bleed navy panel fills the lower 75.5% of the slide, and the orange headline and white body paragraph are centred within it, with the headline above the body. Left and right margins are roughly 15% of the width for the body text. There is no footer, no page number and no logo.

**Visuals**

none. The only non-text element is a flat full-bleed navy rectangle (#001B2B) covering the lower part of the slide, with no image, gradient or overlay.

The title is black (#000000) in VisbyCF-DemiBold, all capitals, with wide letter-spacing, at about 3.65% of slide height (cap height about 2.6%). Typeface is a geometric sans-serif (Visby). Shapes are plain rectangles with square corners, no borders, no shadows and no gradients.

### Slide 17

**Text**

OVERVIEW
Fyre Festival leverages our global access to talent and
our understanding of the millennial demographic to create
THE CULTURAL EXPERIENCE OF THE DECADE.
Fyre Festival is a rich experience that emboldens
our quest to connect a diverse set of influencers.
For two weekends in April and May, the curious and adventurous come
together on a private Exuma island:
Fyre Festival will feature music from the greatest talents in the world,
immersive experiences through art, theatre, and a weekend long treasure
hunt; as well as informative discussions with some of the brightest minds
in the world. Fyre will be defined by its ability to connect: the ultimate in
a tasteful experience.

**Layout**

Single centred column on a full-bleed photograph. The title OVERVIEW sits at the top centre (about 10% from the top). Below it a centred stack runs down the slide: a two-line statement, an orange headline, then a three-paragraph body block ending about 80% down. Text is centre-aligned with no visible footer, page number or logo, and the left and right margins are wide (about 18% to 21%).

**Visuals**

Full-bleed black-and-white photograph covering the whole slide with a dark overlay. Subject: a woman lying back on a dark wooden or resin surface, head tilted back with eyes closed, dark wet hair spilling over the edge, wearing a black swimsuit, left arm bent with hand raised near the chin at right. Background top left is dark, choppy water. A pale grey-white structure, possibly a railing or fitting, fills the right edge from top to bottom. Composition: subject across the centre-left and lower half, head near the upper centre, tight framing with no visible border. Lighting: soft, low-key, mostly shadowed. Dominant colours: near-black, charcoal and grey, with the orange headline as the only colour. Not boxed, not cropped by a frame; no text overlay on the image apart from the typeset copy.

**Design**

Background: Black #000000 with a full-bleed greyscale photograph of a woman lying back, darkened by an overlay. Black background with a greyscale photograph and dark overlay. Title OVERVIEW in white VisbyCF DemiBold capitals with wide letter-spacing, about 3.65% of slide height. Statement lines in white VisbyCF Thin, about 3.09%. Orange headline #F57D21 in VisbyCF DemiBold capitals with wide letter-spacing, about 3.75%. Body text in white VisbyCF Thin, about 2.19%, with bold VisbyCF DemiBold on key phrases (Fyre Festival, Exuma island, music, immersive experiences, treasure hunt, informative discussions). Geometric sans-serif throughout. Lines are loosely spaced. No shapes, borders, gradients, shadows or corner radii.

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph fills the whole slide with no margins. The title block is a centred stack of three lines in the middle of the slide, with a circular play button and a small caption centred below it. There is no footer, slide number or logo.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed black-and-white photograph of a woman's face in close-up, framed from the forehead to the nose. Her wet, shoulder-length blonde hair is slicked back on both sides, and she looks straight at the viewer with her eyes level with the upper third. Her face is the sharp focal point. In the foreground, out of focus and filling the lower half, is a blurred hand or arm reaching across the frame. Small water droplets sit on the bottom edge at the lower right. The lighting is soft and even, with a pale, nearly white background at the top right. The image is grayscale with no tint or overlay. A white outlined circle with a white right-pointing filled triangle (play icon) sits centred below the title, with a thin white outline about 1 px wide and no fill.

**Design**

Background is a full-bleed black-and-white photograph over a black #000000 fill. Title text is white #FFFFFF in VisbyCF DemiBold, a geometric sans-serif, uppercase, with very wide letter-spacing and centre alignment. The title lines are about 6.8 percent of slide height, so the cap height is about 4.8 percent. The caption CLICK TO VIEW is white #FFFFFF in VisbyCF DemiBold, uppercase, widely letter-spaced, at about 1.6 percent of slide height. The play icon is a white thin-line circle with a white filled triangle. No corner radii, shadows or gradients are present.

**Text**

To ignite the Fyre Festival we compiled 400 of the most influential personalities globally to launch a
coordinated influencer marketing campaign.

below accounts reaching over 300mm people in 24 hours.
THESE AMBASSADORS STAND AS PART OF, AND REPRESENTATIVE OF, THE FYRE TRIBE.

**Layout**

A centred single column header sits at the top: the title FYRE STARTERS, then two intro paragraphs, then an orange headline and a closing paragraph. Below, a grid of 14 equal square photographs sits in two rows of seven columns, each photo followed by a centred name and two stat lines; the page has no slide number or footer.

**Visuals**

Fourteen square head-and-shoulders or small-figure photographs in two rows of seven, all boxed with no border, no overlay and no rounded corners. Row 1 left to right: Kendall Jenner, black-and-white close-up of a woman with long straight dark hair looking to the side against a pale grey background; Emily Ratajkowski, colour photo of a woman in a brown fringed crop top with hand in hair against an orange and blue patterned backdrop; Bella Hadid, colour photo of a dark-haired woman in a turquoise bikini against a red-brown wooden wall; Chiara Ferragini, colour street photo of a blonde woman in a black studded leather jacket and black t-shirt with greenery behind; Hailey Baldwin, colour tight crop of a blonde woman with windswept hair in a black tank top against a rust-brown background; Rocky Barnes, colour photo of a woman in a pink kimono-style top and belt bag and sunglasses in front of a green vintage pickup truck; Alessandra Ambrosio, black-and-white portrait of a woman with long wavy hair looking left against a grey background. Row 2 left to right: Nick Bateman, black-and-white photo of a bearded man with hand raised near his face against a dark background; Yovana Ventura, warm-toned close crop of a woman with hand at her chin against a dark background; Bella Thorne, colour full-length view of a woman in a white bikini on a sandy beach with turquoise water and a cloudy blue sky; Marcus Butler, studio headshot of a smiling young man with swept light-brown hair and navy crew-neck top on a white background; Elsa Hosk, colour photo of a woman in a white bikini and sunglasses on sand with blue sky and dunes; Jen Selter, colour photo of a woman in a patterned swimsuit by a turquoise pool with palm trees and blue sky; Paulina Vega, colour close crop of a smiling woman with long brown wavy hair touching her hair against a dark brown background. Lighting is natural daylight in the colour photos and soft even studio light in the headshot.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with black Visby CF sans-serif title and body text in a thin light weight with wide letter-spacing; key phrases in Visby CF DemiBold. The orange headline and link-style names are the only accents: orange #FF7D00 for the headline and royal blue #215E9E for the names, which are bold and underlined. The title is about 3.65 percent of slide height and body text about 1.7 percent; names and stats are about 1.34 percent. Photos are square with no borders or shadows.

**Layout**

Seven-column by three-row grid of square photo tiles, centred horizontally on the slide, each tile followed by a centred underlined name and two centred stat lines. The title block FYRE STARTERS with CONTINUED beneath it is centred at the top, with no footer, no page number and no logo; the grid spans roughly 5.8% to 93.2% of slide width.

**Visuals**

Twenty-one square photographs in a 7 by 3 grid, all same size (about 10% of slide width, about 15% of slide height), boxed with no border, no rounded corners and no shadow. Row 1 (left to right): Ashanti, Anastasia Karanikolaou, Sammy Wilk, Julia Kelly, Bryana Holly, Chanel Iman, Anastasia Ashley. Row 2: Helen Owen, Lais Riberio, Carmella Rose, Kyra Santoro, Shanina Shaik, Nicole Willams, Rose Bertram. Row 3: Steven Kelly, Soo Joo, Joy Corrigan, Hannah Ferguson, Juliana Herz, Marina Laswick, Gizele Oliveira. Ashanti: woman with long black hair in a white top with partial lettering, dark dusk sky, head-and-shoulders. Karanikolaou: woman in orange bikini on turquoise shallow water under blue sky, full figure, bright daylight. Sammy Wilk: young man in light denim jacket and patterned black trousers on grey seamless backdrop, full length, soft studio light, cast shadow on floor. Julia Kelly: woman with long dark hair in black halter top on light grey background, head-and-shoulders, soft light. Bryana Holly: black-and-white portrait of smiling blonde woman with hand at face, white tank top. Chanel Iman: woman with blue-black hair in white tank top, arms crossed, olive textured backdrop, mid shot. Anastasia Ashley: woman in beige swimsuit kneeling on sand before grey-blue sea, overcast light. Helen Owen: smiling woman with wind-blown light brown hair in olive jacket, blurred blue sky, warm light. Lais Riberio: woman in turquoise bikini top and pink bottoms against blue sea and sky, daylight. Carmella Rose: tight close-up of woman with light brown hair and green eyes, red lips, warm brown background, direct gaze. Kyra Santoro: woman with long brown hair in khaki shirt, seated, warm brown background, three-quarter view. Shanina Shaik: tight close-up of face with tanned skin and direct gaze, warm skin tones. Nicole Willams: black-and-white close-up of woman with long dark hair, direct gaze, dark background. Rose Bertram: woman with curly blonde hair in black fur-collar jacket, blurred blue-grey background, looking to side. Steven Kelly: young man with dark curly hair in dark jacket, head-and-shoulders, straight gaze, blurred warm background with orange light spot. Soo Joo: black-and-white photo of woman with long hair, hand at chin, dark jacket. Joy Corrigan: blonde woman in orange swimsuit leaning on the edge of a blue-green pool with sunlight reflections. Hannah Ferguson: woman in white top on pale beach background, soft natural light. Juliana Herz: close-up of woman with long blonde hair in dark top, navy background, warm light. Marina Laswick: woman with long light brown hair in black top and jeans, seated among dry shrubs in autumn tones. Gizele Oliveira: woman in black wide-brim hat, black choker and black-and-white patterned top against light grey background. Photographs are not cropped beyond square; no overlays or tints are visible. Lighting is mostly soft daylight or studio light.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with black title and thin black subtitle in a geometric sans-serif (Visby). Names are bold blue #215E9E in Gotham-style sans-serif, underlined. Stat lines are light grey-black #000000 in Gotham Light with wide letter-spacing. Title about 2.9% of slide height, subtitle about 1.9%, names and stats about 1.3% of slide height. A cyan #00AEEF rectangle sits behind the top-right photo position and is covered by the photograph. No shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

**Layout**

Single white slide with a centred title block at the top (about 10 to 16 percent of the height) and a grid of 14 creator portraits in two centred rows of seven below it, each portrait followed by a centred name and two centred stat lines. Rows sit at about 27.7 and 54.6 percent of the height, columns are evenly spaced at roughly 12.6 percent of the width, and the grid leaves wide empty margins. No footer, logo, date or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Fourteen square photographs, each about 10 percent of slide width and 15 percent of slide height, arranged in two rows of seven, all unboxed, unrounded and uncropped to a square. Row 1, left to right: Daniella Lopez, a woman in a royal-blue sweater seated against a white wall in daylight; Nadine Leopold, a woman with long light-brown hair and a black lace bra, soft neutral studio backdrop; Cindy Prado, a woman with long blonde hair and a purple top against a beige wall; Ryan Leslie, a smiling man with glasses and a black leather jacket in dim warm light with an orange-brown background; Chris Harris Jr., an American football player in an orange and navy Denver-style uniform with number 25 in a stadium with other players behind; Delilah Belle, a woman with long blonde hair and a dark top against a pale blue-grey background; Keilah K, a woman with long dark hair in a turquoise bikini top beside a white boat on a turquoise sea. Row 2, left to right: Duckie Thot, a woman with hands behind her head in a white shirt against a white background; Chris Johnson, a football player in a light-blue jersey with dreadlocks and a black headband raising one finger against a grey smoky background; Joe Weir, a man in a sage-green tank top against a grey backdrop; Anna Herrin, a woman with blonde hair in a black and white sports bra on a warm beige background; Stipe Miocic, a shirtless fighter with arms raised against a dark background; Brooks Nader, a woman in sunglasses and a black halter top against a city skyline; Tatiana Dieteman, a woman with brown hair touching her temple against a dark grey background. Lighting is mostly even studio or daylight. Two solid #00AEEF blue rectangles, each about 9.9 by 14.8 percent, lie exactly behind the photos at Duckie Thot's position and Keilah K's position and are fully hidden by the opaque images. Names are blue underlined links.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in a wide-tracked geometric sans-serif capitals, bold weight, black, about 2.9 percent of slide height. Subtitle CONTINUED in light, thin geometric sans-serif capitals, black, about 2 percent of slide height. Creator names in bold geometric humanist sans-serif, #215E9E, underlined, about 1.3 percent of slide height. Stat lines in light geometric sans-serif with generous letter-spacing, black, about 1.3 percent of slide height. No shapes, borders, shadows or gradients are visible; photos are flat with no corner radius.

**Layout**

Centred title block at the top, above a grid of fourteen equal square portraits arranged in two rows of seven columns, each portrait centred over its name and two stat lines. Rows are evenly spaced in the middle of the slide with generous white margins on all sides; there is no footer, logo or slide number.

**Visuals**

Fourteen square headshot photographs, each boxed at about 10 percent of slide width, with no border, shadow or overlay. Top row, left to right: Lyzy Adler, a young woman with long light-brown hair looking up and sideways in front of blurred teal water with warm skin tones; Cassie Brown, a smiling young woman with long blonde hair in a grey Adidas-logo sweatshirt in front of a dark green foliage background; Maggie Rawlins, a smiling woman with wavy blonde hair in a dark striped halter top with a hand near her chin against a light grey backdrop; Heidy De La Rosa, a woman with straight black hair in a black off-shoulder top against a white backdrop; Alina Baikova, a woman with voluminous auburn curls in a plaid jacket against a white backdrop; Marshall Faulk, a smiling bald man in a grey suit, white shirt and yellow tie with a yellow lapel flower against a grey backdrop; Aisha Atkins, a smiling woman with straight black hair and pink lipstick against a dark blue background. Bottom row, left to right: Corey Liuget, a man with a serious expression in a blue collared top against a red-and-white background, in grey-toned colour; Natalia Borges, a woman with brown hair falling over one eye, bare shoulders, against a dark brown backdrop; Brittany Atkins, a woman with a large curly dark afro in a pink satin top and dark fur wrap against a beige backdrop; Ania C, a woman with long wavy blonde hair in a white top against a pale beige backdrop; Anggie Bryan, a black-and-white portrait of a woman with long wavy dark hair and bare shoulders; Alan Leyva, a smiling young man in a white t-shirt holding a red strap in both hands against a light grey backdrop; Brandon Flowers, a man with short hair and a beard looking to the side with a dark blurred background. No charts, tables, diagrams or logos.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with black tracked capital title and light grey-black thin subtitle in a geometric sans-serif (Visby CF style). Names are bold blue #215E9E in a geometric sans-serif (Gotham-style) with underline; stats are light black #000000 in Gotham Light with wide letter-spacing. Title is about 2.9 percent of slide height in demi-bold caps; subtitle about 1.95 percent in thin caps; name and stat text about 1.34 percent of slide height. Shapes are plain rectangles with square corners, no shadows, no gradients.

**Layout**

Two-column split at about 50% of the width. The left half is a collage of six photographs in a three-row grid: a narrow column of three equal strips at the far left edge (x 0 to 16.7%), and a wider column beside it (x 16.5 to 50.1%) with one tall photo over one smaller photo. The right half is white with a centred text block. The title FYRE STARTERS sits centred above the body copy at about 14% from the top. The body copy is centred in a block of about 58 to 92% of the width, and the Instagram screenshot sits below it, centred at about 57 to 93% horizontally and 51 to 86% vertically. There is no footer and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Six photographs and one screenshot, plus a comic burst graphic. (1) Top-left strip, black-and-white street scene: a group of young people on a pavement, a woman in a white fluffy coat holding a small object at her chest, with a yellow comic-style POP! burst overlapping the right edge of the photo near its lower right. (2) Middle-left strip, black-and-white: a tall palm tree with a figure on a striped hammock-like curved form, cloudy sky, small palm fronds at the bottom. (3) Lower-left strip, black-and-white: a woman with long dark hair, a jewelled necklace and bracelet, a sleeveless dark top, against a pale sky, one hand on her hip, full-bleed in its cell. (4) Large centre photo, colour, full height of the top two-thirds of the collage: a woman with long, wavy dark hair in a black velvet blazer with satin lapels, a black bandeau top, a gold choker-style collar and a gold embellished belt over a black satin skirt, standing in front of a grey panelled wall with small round studs, looking at the camera, soft even daylight, dark and grey palette with warm skin tones and gold accents, cropped at the bottom edge. (5) Lower centre photo, warm sepia-toned: two women lying on white bedding in patterned strapless tops, one with her hand raised to her head, photographs and frames overlapping, out-of-focus string lights at the top right, cropped at the bottom edge. (6) Instagram post screenshot, boxed with a thin light-grey border, about 1.3 times wider than tall: left half a black-and-white beach photo of two women with palm fronds over their heads, a flat orange-yellow rectangle behind them and the figures tinted orange, grey sea and sand; right half a white panel showing a small circular profile photo, the username kendalljenner, a blue outlined Follow button, the line 5,913,247 views with 5d at right, the caption text in small dark grey type with @fyrefestival and #fyrefestival in blue, the line view all 30,087 comments, a list of comments, and a blue Log in to like or comment. line at the foot with three small grey dots.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF with no texture. Title in VisbyCF DemiBold, a geometric sans-serif, wide letter-spacing, all caps, black #000000, about 3.7% of the slide height for the cap height region. Body copy in VisbyCF Thin, geometric sans-serif, all caps, generous letter-spacing, line spacing about 2.1 times the font size, black #000000, about 1.7% of the slide height. The one emphasised phrase, 6 MILLION UNIQUE IMPRESSIONS, is set in VisbyCF DemiBold, black. Photos are unframed, no rounded corners, no shadows. Screenshot has a thin light-grey #D9D9D9 border. Accent colours are the mustard-yellow #E0A526 of the POP! burst and the orange overlay in the screenshot, and the blue #3897F0 of the Follow button and links. No lines or rules on the text side.

**Layout**

Single white slide with a centred composition. The title sits centred at the top, followed by a centred two-line intro and a centred stat line, then a three-row grid of 13 publication logos (row 1 five logos, row 2 five logos, row 3 three logos centred), and below that two centred orange quote columns, left and right of the slide centre. No footer, no slide number, margins roughly 5 to 12 percent of the slide width.

**Visuals**

13 black-and-white or black publication logos on white, no photographs or charts. Row 1 left to right: VOGUE (serif, high-contrast black wordmark); ELLE AUSTRALIA (serif wordmark with small tracked AUSTRALIA beneath); COSMOPOLITAN (heavy black condensed sans wordmark); Esquire (black bold script wordmark); BAZAAR with small Harper’s mark above the right end (black Didone-style wordmark). Row 2 left to right: InStyle (dark navy #0B1F3A-like sans wordmark with a descender on the y); TMZ (black bold sans with a T and M and Z in heavy blocks); Sports Illustrated (black slab-style stacked wordmark); POPSUGAR. (widely tracked dark grey sans capitals with a full stop); GLAMOUR (heavy black condensed sans wordmark). Row 3 centred: PLAYBOY (black rabbit head logo above a bold serif PLAYBOY wordmark); W magazine (black thin-stroke serif W monogram); FOX NEWS.com (white FOX NEWS lettering on black square with grey .com bar beneath and a diagonal slash mark). All logos are flat, unboxed, with no shadows or overlays.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean minimal white design. Title in VisbyCF-DemiBold, uppercase, wide letter-spacing, black #000000. Intro and stat lines in VisbyCF-DemiBold, black #000000, with tracked lowercase for the intro. Highlight figures in the stat line are orange #F47B20. Quote blocks are VisbyCF-DemiBold in orange #FF7D00, centred, with blank-line spacing between quotes. Typeface is a geometric sans-serif. Title is about 3.65 percent of slide height, stat line about 2.43, intro about 1.70, quotes about 2.19. No lines, shapes, corner radii, shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Full-bleed dark concert photograph with a centred single-column stack of text. The title sits at the top centre (y 10.3%) and the stats and statement run down the centre axis, with the stat pairs and the closing phrase centred on the vertical midline. No footer, page number or logo; margins are not fixed beyond the centred column.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed photograph covering the entire slide. Subject: a crowd at a live event seen from behind, with a raised hand silhouetted in the left-centre and a head and shoulder silhouetted at the lower right. A bright stage light, lens flare and a large screen glow at the centre-left, with a dim round light fixture at centre-right. Composition: the camera is placed among the crowd looking toward the stage, the image is cropped to the frame edges, and the photo is not boxed. Lighting: a single strong spotlight source behind the crowd, the rest in deep shadow. Dominant colours: near-black, slate blue-grey (about #5C6E7C in the highlights) and white glare. A dark blue-grey tint overlays the photo, and the lower left and right are heavily darkened. No charts, tables, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background is a very dark navy #001B2B with a full-bleed concert photograph on top that is tinted dark blue-grey. Title colour is orange #FF7D00. Body and stat text is white #FFFFFF. Accent colour is orange #FF7D00 used only for the title. Typeface is VisbyCF DemiBold, a geometric sans-serif, set in capitals with wide letter-spacing. Title size is about 6.4% of slide height (cap height roughly 4.5%). Large stat numbers are about 4.3% of slide height and the captions below them are about 1.6%; the statement lines are about 4.3% and the small SECURING label is about 1.7%. Line shapes and corner radii: none. Shadows, gradients on shapes: none.

### Slide 28

**Text**

BRAND SYNERGY

**Layout**

A single centred title sits across the middle of the slide, horizontally centred and slightly below the vertical midpoint, with no footer, slide number, logo or other furniture. The image runs full-bleed to all four edges with no margins.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed photograph covers the whole slide: a close-up, top-down view of a brain-coral surface, with meandering ridges and dense spiny texture across the frame. It is rendered in greyscale and heavily darkened, so the ridges read as charcoal grey (about #2A2A2A) against near-black shadows, with no visible subject or action. Lighting is flat and even, composition is an all-over pattern with no focal point, and the image is not cropped to a shape. A dark overlay or tint darkens the photo over the whole slide.

**Design**

Background: Greyscale coral-surface photograph darkened over a solid black #000000 fill. Full-bleed dark photographic treatment on a black base, with the orange title as the only colour on the slide. The title is set in VisbyCF DemiBold, a geometric sans-serif, in all capitals with wide letter-spacing, at about 6.8% of slide height. There are no shapes, lines, borders, corner radii, shadows or gradients beyond the darkening of the photograph.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- photo at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#2A2A2A]: Greyscale close-up photograph of brain-coral surface with meandering ridges and spiny texture, darkened with an overlay (Full-bleed, not cropped to a shape, no border, no rotation, darkened by a dark tint so the ridges read as dim grey; covers the black fill and sits behind the title.)

### Slide 29

**Text**

Sponsorship is a key revenue stream for Fyre Festival.
Our 360 methodology allows to capture brand revenue in a unique manner
Understand Brand Goals
Ideate
Conceptualize
Execute

**Layout**

Centered, symmetrical composition with no visible grid. Two intro lines sit at the top, centered at about x 50. Four process labels form a staggered flow down the slide: Understand Brand Goals is top centre, Ideate and Conceptualize sit on a middle row, and Execute is bottom centre. There is no visible title placeholder, footer, logo or slide number, and the content is set with generous margins on all sides.

**Visuals**

Diagram, a four-step process flow with orange connectors and no photographs or icons. Shapes: four text labels, each underlined by a solid orange bar (Understand Brand Goals, Ideate, Conceptualize, Execute). Connectors: (1) an elbow arrow from the underline below Understand Brand Goals runs straight down, turns left and runs down to a small downward arrowhead above Ideate. (2) A straight horizontal arrow runs from the right end of Ideate to a rightward arrowhead just before Conceptualize. (3) An elbow arrow runs straight down from the underline below Conceptualize, turns left and runs down to a downward arrowhead above Execute. All connectors are thin orange lines of about 1 px with small solid triangular arrowheads. No chart, table or photograph.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #001B2B. Flat dark navy background with no gradient or texture. Text is white #FFFFFF in VisbyCF DemiBold, a geometric humanist sans-serif. The intro line is set in a wide letter-spacing, about 0.05 em. Step labels are set in regular spacing, about 3.9 percent of slide height, and the second intro line is about 1.7 percent. Accent colour is orange #FF7F00 for underlines, connectors and arrowheads. Underlines are solid bars about 0.45 percent of slide height thick with square ends. Connectors are 1 px lines with no shadows, corner radii or rounded ends.

### Slide 30

**Text**

PENDING PARTNERSHIPS / Fyre Festival has identified and secured several brand partners that will attract the aspirational, thrill-seeking audience that the island deserves. / By amplifying our marketing efforts through their existing channels, we will in turn be able to elevate the onsite experience for all. / OUR PENDING PARTNERS INCLUDE: / PENDING / PENDING / PENDING / PENDING / PENDING / CONFIRMED / CONFIRMED / CONFIRMED

**Layout**

Two horizontal bands. The top band is white with a centred title at about 10% from the top, two centred body lines and a centred bold subhead, all on a single centre axis. The lower band from about 34% to the bottom edge is a full-bleed dark photograph carrying a 3-column, 3-row grid of partner logos, each with a centred uppercase status label beneath it. Row 1 holds Samsung, Vilebrequin and Casamigos Tequila; row 2 holds Pernod Ricard on the left, an empty centre cell and Bandier on the right; row 3 holds Magnises, Snapchat and Tablelist. No footer, slide number or logo appears outside the grid.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed dark photograph occupying the lower band from 33.8% to 100% of slide height and the full width. It shows a dark silhouette of a performer standing behind a keyboard on an X-frame stand, centred slightly left, with the head partly obscured by the Vilebrequin logo. Hazy, smoky background with faint stage lights at right and dim silhouettes of a crowd and tent-like shapes at the bottom. Lighting is low-key and backlit, dominant colours near-black #0B0F12 and blue-grey #2A3138, with soft warm highlights. The image is not boxed and no tint is visible beyond its natural darkness. Logos (all orange #FC8002 unless noted): Samsung wordmark in black #000000 on an orange #FC8002 ellipse tilted upward; VILEBREQUIN wordmark in orange with a slanted, arched letterform; CASAMIGOS Tequila lockup with a fan-shaped agave emblem above the wordmark and script Tequila below; Pernod Ricard lockup with a sunburst emblem above a serif wordmark; BANDIER geometric wordmark with an angled A; MAGNISES wordmark with a stylised X-mark at left and widely tracked letters; Snapchat ghost outline icon in orange line; TABLELIST wordmark with a stylised double-T mark at left.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF in the top band; dark full-bleed photograph (near-black #0B0F12 with blue-grey haze) in the lower band over a #001B2B fill. Top band background is white #FFFFFF; lower photograph is dark. Title is black #000000 in a geometric sans-serif (Visby CF DemiBold), uppercase, letter-spaced, about 3.65% of slide height. Body text is black #000000 in Visby CF Thin, about 1.70% of slide height, lowercase, widely letter-spaced. Subhead is black #000000 in Visby CF DemiBold uppercase at about 1.70%. Status labels are white #FFFFFF in Visby CF DemiBold uppercase at about 2.19%, letter-spaced. Accent colour is orange #FC8002 used for all logos. Shapes: the Samsung oval is a filled orange ellipse; no corner radii, shadows or gradients on text. The dark band fill is #001B2B under the photograph.

### Slide 31

**Text**

LAND OWNERSHIP
Fyre has been given $8.4mm of market value land on Black Point, Exuma
in exchange for hosting the festival and advertising the island.

**Layout**

Full-bleed photograph fills the slide with a dark overlay. A single centred title sits at about 24% from the top, and a centred two-line body paragraph sits at about 50% to 55% of the height, over the turquoise water to the left of the beach. There is no grid, footer, slide number or logo, and the text block is centred horizontally on the slide.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed aerial drone photograph of a low, narrow Bahamian island coastline, probably Black Point, Exuma. Turquoise shallow water fills the lower-left and centre. A pale pink-beige sand beach with a white surf line curves from the upper-left down to the lower-right. Dark green scrubby vegetation covers the upper-middle and the entire right side. Further islands and shallow lagoon water lie along the top and upper-right, and a dark grey overcast sky fills the top band. A small red-orange structure is partly visible at the right edge at about x 97, y 34 to 36. Lighting is flat and overcast. Dominant colours are turquoise #1E9FA3, sand #D8BFB0, dark olive green #1F2F1A and slate grey #3A4048. The photo is uncropped apart from full bleed, with no border. A dark navy overlay (#001B2B) and a black layer (#000000) sit over the whole photo, darkening it, and the opacity is not recorded.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed aerial photograph of a coastline, darkened by a navy #001B2B and black #000000 overlay. Photographic background with a dark navy and black tint over the entire slide, giving a moody, subdued look. The title and body text are white #FFFFFF. The title is set in Visby CF DemiBold, a geometric sans-serif, in capital letters with wide letter-spacing, at about 4% of slide height. The body is set in Visby CF DemiBold in sentence case with slight letter-spacing, at about 2.2% of slide height. No accent colours, lines, shapes or corner radii are present.

- Suggested repair: Raise body text size and weight and contrast on slides 3, 4, 10 and 36 to 40, and give the raise, financials and use of funds real content instead of one-line slides 32 and 33.

### Slide 36

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Portrait headshots paired with short bios give each founder a clear, consistent card-like structure.
- Review: But much of the information sits in hairline, low-contrast grey type that is barely legible even at readable size (slides 3, 4, 10, 36 to 40), and the contact sheets show the same problem on the problem, vision and overview slides.
- Suggested repair: Raise body text size and weight and contrast on slides 3, 4, 10 and 36 to 40, and give the raise, financials and use of funds real content instead of one-line slides 32 and 33.

**Text**

FOUNDERS / BILLY MCFARLAND / FOUNDER & CEO / Billy McFarland is the Founder and CEO of Fyre, where he guides the company’s overall direction and strategy. His other companies include Magnises and Spling. Billy is also the founder of two non-profits Accelerate and LEAP. / JA RULE / FOUNDER / Ja Rule is the founder of Fyre, where he is responsible for overall business strategy, guiding creative and facilitating artist relations. A three time Grammy Nominee with over 60 million records sold worldwide, he is widely recognized as among the most commercially successful hip hop artists of all time. / In the tech space, he is an investor and advisor to the hugely successful members-only benefits platform, Magnises. And as a television personality he is both star and executive prouder of the MTV docu-series Follow The Rules.

**Layout**

Single centred column on a white page. The title FOUNDERS sits centred horizontally near the top (about 9% from the top edge). Two founder entries are stacked vertically in the lower two-thirds: each has a small square portrait on the left (left edge about 30% of the width) and a left-aligned text block to its right (text starts about 39% of the width, lines run to about 69%). Entries are separated by a large vertical gap; the Billy entry starts at about 24% of the height and the Ja Rule entry at about 46%. No footer, no page number, no logos. Left and right margins are roughly equal, about 30% each side.

**Visuals**

Two founder portraits, both head-and-shoulders photographs, boxed as square-cornered rectangles with no border or shadow, not cropped to a circle. Behind the Billy McFarland portrait sits a flat cream rectangle (#F9F5EF) slightly larger than the photo (about 0.1% of width and 0.5% of height bigger on each side), so the photo appears framed on a cream field. Billy McFarland photo: a smiling young man with short brown hair, open white collared shirt with grey shading, looking at camera, soft even daylight, light off-white background, framed from chest up with the head near the upper third. Ja Rule photo: a man with a shaved head and a serious, slightly frowning expression, wearing a blue checked blazer over a white t-shirt, looking at camera, even studio lighting, plain light grey to white background, framed from chest up. Both photos are low resolution (109 by 131 pixels and 111 by 111 pixels) and look soft. No overlays or tints.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF). All text is pure black (#000000) with no accent colour. Title FOUNDERS is set in a geometric sans-serif, Visby CF DemiBold, all capitals with wide letter-spacing, at about 3.65% of slide height (cap height about 2.5%). Names are set in Visby CF DemiBold capitals with slight letter-spacing at about 1.46% of slide height. Role lines and biographies are set in Visby CF Thin, sentence case, with generous letter-spacing and line spacing of about 2.7% of slide height (about 1.8 times the font size). Body text is about 1.46% of slide height. Only shapes are the single cream rectangle behind the first portrait; no lines, borders, corner radii, shadows or gradients.

**Review notes**

**Text**

THE FYRE SQUAD
MDAVID LOW
CCO
MDavid Low is a co-founder of Fyre and its
Chief Creative Officer, responsible for product design
excellence and implementation. Prior to Fyre, MD created
notable and award winning digital products as Executive
Creative Director at R/GA as well as Design Director at Nike.
GRANT MARGOLIN
CMO
Grant oversees marketing and brand strategy. Prior to his
role with Fyre, he oversaw all marketing and brand strategy
for Magnises, the fastest growing experiential benefits
platform globally. Grant began his career working for
Daymond John, a noted angel investor and “Shark” on
ABC’s Shark Tank. Grant graduated Summa Cum Laude
from Syracuse University’s Bandier Program for Music and
Allied Entertainment Industries.
MARK NAUROTH
CTO
Mark oversees the architecture and implementation of Fyre’s
digital platforms. His career writing enterprise-level software
spans sectors as diverse as finance and food service and has
grossed tens of millions of revenue. Mark is also an author,
speaker, and avid antiquities collector.
JASON VE
CRO
Jason oversees revenue-generating functions at Fyre,
including sales, talent bookings, business development
and partnerships. Jason has a decade of experience
in the music, entertainment and tech industry, and
comes to Fyre from Google where he oversaw music
and entertainment partnerships. Prior to Google, Jason
worked in business development roles at Disney and
Viacom, and as an investment banker at UBS. Jason
graduated from NYU Stern.
MICHAEL CICCARELLI
LEAD, FRONT-END
Michael is responsible for both front-end and
back-end engineering. Michael was previously
the Lead Engineer at Vice Media and Condé Nast.
SHIYUAN DENG
PRODUCT DESIGNER
Shiyuan is responsible for product design,
working with both the creative and engineering
team to design meaningful features for Fyre’s users.
Shiyuan’s entrepreneurial background allows her
to understand both Fyre’s product goals and
user’s needs.

**Layout**

Title centred horizontally at the top, about 13% from the top edge. Two columns of team entries below: the left column starts at x 16% with a 6% square photo at x 8% to its left; the right column starts at x 62.8% with its photo at x 55%. Each entry is a photo beside a name, role and bio block, left-aligned, with the photos top-aligned to the name lines. The left column holds MDavid Low, Grant Margolin and Mark Nauroth; the right column holds Jason Ve, Michael Ciccarelli and Shiyuan Deng. No footer, page number or logo is visible; margins are about 8% on the left and 10% on the right.

**Visuals**

Six square head-and-shoulders portrait photographs, each boxed with no border or rounded corners, one per team member. Left column, top to bottom: MDavid Low, a black-and-white close-up of a bearded man in aviator sunglasses and headphones against a grey background, cropped at the shoulders. Grant Margolin, a smiling man with dark wavy hair and black-rimmed glasses in a light grey setting, in colour and softly lit. Mark Nauroth, a smiling man with glasses and a teal shirt against a white background, in colour with even front lighting. Right column, top to bottom: Jason Ve, a smiling man in a dark jacket and white collared shirt against a saturated green background, in colour. Michael Ciccarelli, a man in a dark shirt looking slightly off-camera against a near-black background, dimly lit. Shiyuan Deng, a woman with dark hair and glasses against a dark brown background with warm, muted tones. No logos or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background, minimal and modern. ### Slide 38

**Review notes**

- Review: But much of the information sits in hairline, low-contrast grey type that is barely legible even at readable size (slides 3, 4, 10, 36 to 40), and the contact sheets show the same problem on the problem, vision and overview slides.
- Suggested repair: Raise body text size and weight and contrast on slides 3, 4, 10 and 36 to 40, and give the raise, financials and use of funds real content instead of one-line slides 32 and 33.

**Text**

THE FYRE SQUAD
SHERIDAN LOW
DIGITAL PRODUCTION MANAGER
Sheridan Low is responsible for digital production
and live stream operations. Prior to Fyre, Sheridan
proudly served in the Army National Guard as the
Operations and Logistics Sergeant for California’s
Search and Extraction team within the Homeland
Response Force.
HO WILKERSON
DIRECTOR, TALENT & BOOKING OPERATIONS
HO oversees all operations for Fyre’s bookings,
liaising with the talent team, marketing team and
buyer to ensure a seamless booking. Prior to Fyre he
ran marketing and operations for Murder Inc. and
Bad Boy Entertainment.
NYLA COFFIE
DIRECTOR OF EXPERIENTIAL MARKETING
Nyla oversees experiential marketing. Her goal is to
bring the Fyre brand to life for all of our audiences.
Prior to her role with Fyre, she led event marketing
for the NYC region of Breather. She has also
worked in event marketing at Pager and Teach For
America.
IAN BROWNE
TALENT DIRECTOR
Ian is Manager of Talent at Fyre, leading model
and buyer acquisition. Prior to Fyre, Ian was COO
of Patrick McMullan Agency where he led the joint
venture with Getty Images. Prior to his role with PMC,
Ian started the capital raising division at Phoenix
Partners Group, an inter dealer broker in NYC. Ian
has an MBA from Emory University
SAMUEL KROST
DIRECTOR, TALENT MANAGEMENT
Samuel oversees talent relationships and bookings
for Fyre. Samuel’s industry background at Helmut
Lang and Onia allows him to develop meaningful
relationships with both talent and buyers.
GRACIE LUNDELL
PRODUCT ENGINEER
Gracie is a developer for front-end design for Fyre.
Gracie was previously a front-end developer for
Blacktower Entertainment and graduated from
Portland State University.

**Layout**

Single centred title at the top of the slide (centred near x 50%, y 13% to 16%) over a two-column grid of three stacked person entries per column. The left column starts at x 8% with a photo at its left edge and text beginning at x 16%; the right column starts at x 54.7% with photo and text beginning at x 62.8%. Each entry is a square headshot at left, with name, role and bio stacked to its right, left aligned. Entries are spaced at roughly 22% to 23% of slide height in the left column and 23% in the right column, with the left column rows at y 25.5%, 47.1% and 73.8%. There is no footer, page number or logo, and the margins are about 8% on the left and right.

**Visuals**

Six square head-and-shoulders headshots, one per person, each placed as a small unboxed photo with no border, overlay or tint. Sheridan Low: a man with short light-brown hair and a dark jacket, facing the camera with a slight smile, against a pale blue-grey window background, lit evenly by daylight. Ho Wilkerson: a man wearing a black cap, dark jacket and looking toward the camera, against a blue sky background at upper right, with strong contrast on the face. Nyla Coffie: a smiling woman with shoulder-length dark hair in a dark grey blazer over a magenta top, against a plain white background, evenly lit. Ian Browne: a man with dark hair and a white open-collared shirt, three-quarter view, against a near-black background with soft lighting. Samuel Krost: a black and white photograph of a man with short dark hair looking off to one side, against a black background with high contrast. Gracie Lundell: a smiling woman with light brown hair pulled back, wearing a red top, against a warm orange-brown background with soft lighting. Photos are not cropped beyond square framing, sit at head-and-shoulders scale, and are not full bleed.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white slide with no decoration, gradients or shadows. Title is set in widely letter-spaced uppercase DemiBold sans-serif, black #000000, at about 3.65% of slide height. Person names are uppercase DemiBold sans-serif at about 1.46% of slide height, black #000000, letter-spaced. Roles are uppercase Thin sans-serif, black #000000, letter-spaced. Bios are Thin sans-serif in sentence case, black #000000, widely letter-spaced, at about 1.46% of slide height with line pitch of about 2.7% of slide height. No accent colours; the only colours are the black type and the photographs. The typeface is Visby CF, a geometric sans-serif, in DemiBold and Thin weights.

**Text**

THE FYRE SQUAD
MATTE
CREATIVE PARTNER
MATTE is a creative agency and production company focused on culture and content creation.
Some of our clients include CHANEL, DKNY FINISH LINE, GOOGLE HELMUT LANG, INTERVIEW MAJE MARGIELA, NIKE, PERNOD RICARD, RAG & BONE, SAMSUNG, SNAPCHAT, SOHO HOUSE, VOGUE.
FUCK JERRY
SOCIAL MEDIA
From the success of his personal instagram account, Elliot Tebele has built an empire through social media. Through thoughtful content strategy, FuckJerry references in-depth analytics to ensure that the data behind content performance is aligned with creative direction. Analytics are based on three factors: audience, engagement and optimization.
42WEST
PUBLIC RELATIONS
With unparalleled experience, contacts, and expertise, 42West is one of the leading full-service public-relations firms in the entertainment industry.

**Layout**

Three equal columns of text and images sit below a centred title, with the title centred near the top of the slide at about 10 percent of the slide height. Each column is about 14 percent of the slide wide, starting at x 24.9, 42.8 and 60.8 percent; each column has an image or logo at top, then a bold name, a thin caps role line, and a body paragraph left-aligned beneath. The first two columns have images of equal top edge near y 23.6 percent; the 42West logo sits at the same top band. No footer, page number or source line is visible.

**Visuals**

Three images. (1) Column one: a square black (#0A0A0A) panel with the white thin sans-serif wordmark MATTE set in capitals across the centre, full width of the panel, boxed with hard square corners, no shadow. (2) Column two: a black-and-white photograph of a man with short dark hair under a dark flat cap, wearing a light shirt with a small dot pattern, one hand raised to his chest, looking to the viewer's left; head and torso framed in a slightly cropped rectangle, soft natural light from the left, greyscale with white and light grey tones, the image edges are not outlined. (3) Column three: an orange (#F28C1E) filled circle with a darker orange rim, containing the white numerals 42 in large bold sans-serif and the word WEST in white capitals below the numerals, on a white background, no shadow.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background (#FFFFFF). Title in Visby CF DemiBold, black #000000, all capitals, wide letter-spacing, about 3.65 percent of slide height. Role lines and body copy in Visby CF Thin, black #000000, capitals for role lines and clients list, sentence case for body, about 1.46 percent of slide height with generous line spacing (about 2.3 percent between lines). Centre: the Institute logo, the white uppercase thin sans-serif wordmark INSTITUTE centred on a pure black (#000000) square. A #202020 filled rectangle sits behind the DPS image at the same size, forming the square backing. No photographs, charts or tables.

### Slide 41

**Review notes**

- Worth keeping: Keep the navy and orange palette, the one-word divider slides and the full-bleed atmospheric photography on slides 13, 14 and 41.

**Text**

_(no text on this slide)_

**Layout**

Full-bleed six-photo mosaic with no margins, gutters or title; three columns of equal width (each about 33.3% of slide width). The left and right columns each hold a tall photo at the lower two-thirds plus a short photo above, and the centre column holds a tall photo at the top with a short photo below, so the tall images interlock in a staggered pattern. No slide number, footer or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Six square-edged colour and black-and-white photographs of the sea and people, arranged as a mosaic. (1) Top-left, a landscape crop: a woman's legs and arms rise out of shallow sea, splashing spray in the air, warm sunlit water, muted sky and a green shoreline at left; warm golden tones. (2) Top-centre, a tall portrait crop: a dark jagged rock outcrop rises from white surf under a pale grey-blue sky, with a small figure on the peak; cool blue-grey and teal tones. (3) Top-right, a landscape crop in black and white: a woman lies on her side on sand in a halter bikini, one arm raised over her head, looking toward the camera, with soft contrast and a light grey background. (4) Bottom-left, a tall portrait crop: a woman with long blonde hair tilts her face up toward the sun with eyes closed, a small gold cross earring, a lens flare at upper right, pale blue sky and bright turquoise water; warm skin tones. (5) Bottom-centre, a landscape crop in black and white: a surfer crouches on a surfboard on a breaking wave, spray flying around, grey tonal background. (6) Right, a tall portrait crop: a woman in a dark bikini is mid-backflip above turquoise sea with arms outstretched, and a weathered wooden post in the bottom-left foreground; saturated blue-green water with white wave foam at top. All photos appear full-bleed inside their cells with no borders, captions or overlays, and each is cropped at its cell edge.

**Design**

Background: Not visible; the six photographs cover the entire slide edge to edge, with no solid background colour showing. No typography and no graphic elements. The mosaic uses a mix of full-colour and monochrome photographs; the three black-and-white images (top-right, bottom-centre and the monochrome sand scene) sit against the colour images for contrast. Photos meet edge to edge with no gaps, borders or rounded corners, and no shadows or gradients are visible.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- photo at x 0, y 0, w 33.3, h 33.5 [#D9A56B]: Top-left photo: woman's legs and arms splashing out of shallow sea, warm sunlit spray, green shoreline at left (Full-bleed rectangular crop, no border or overlay; dominant colours warm golden-brown and pale blue-grey; sunlit highlights on water.)
- photo at x 33.3, y 0, w 33.3, h 66.8 [#2E4A55]: Tall centre-top photo: dark jagged sea rock outcrop with white surf and grey-blue sky (Full-bleed rectangular crop, no border or overlay; dominant colours slate teal, pale grey and white; rock occupies the centre-lower part of the frame.)

### Slide 42

**Text**

“SEEK THOSE WHO LIGHT YOUR FLAMES.”
-RUMI

**Layout**

A single centred quotation set across two lines in the vertical middle of a full-bleed photograph: the first line spans nearly the full slide width with its left edge near 6.7% and right edge near 93%, and the attribution '-RUMI' sits centred below it. The text is centred horizontally with no visible title, footer, page number or margin frame.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed photograph covering the entire slide (1360 by 917 px). Subject: a live concert crowd at night. In the centre a bright white stage light and lens-flare starburst glows behind a tall dark vertical silhouette of a performer, with a lit stage screen to the left of the glow. In the lower left a raised hand and arm of a silhouetted audience member, with more raised hands further left; in the lower right the dark silhouetted head and shoulders of another audience member, cropped at the right and bottom edges. Scattered particles and confetti-like specks fill the dark upper area. Composition: centre-weighted with the light source at the middle, figures framed at the bottom. Lighting: a single bright backlight from the stage, otherwise very dark. Dominant colours: near-black navy and blue-grey (monochrome). The photo is cropped to the slide edges and has a dark blue-grey tint overlay over it, which makes it look monochrome.

**Design**

Background is a deep navy-black #001B2B with a monochrome blue-grey photograph over it. The main quotation is orange #FF7D00 in a geometric sans-serif typeface (Visby CF DemiBold), set in capitals with wide letter-spacing and weight DemiBold. The small connecting words 'WHO' and 'YOUR' are white #FFFFFF in the same typeface, much smaller, in capitals. The opening and closing quotation marks and the full stop are white #FFFFFF. The attribution '-RUMI' is white #FFFFFF, DemiBold capitals, in the same size as the main quote. Main text is about 6.8% of slide height (cap height roughly 4.7%). Small words are about 3.0% of slide height. No shapes, lines, shadows, corner radii or gradients are visible.

### Slide 43

**Text**

FYRE
FYREAPP.COM

**Layout**

Single centred logo lockup on a plain white field with no title, body text or grid; the mark stack sits at the horizontal centre and vertical middle of the slide, with a small URL line centred near the bottom edge. No footer or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Logo lockup, stacked vertically from top to bottom: (1) a solid black flame icon with a single white curved highlight line down its right side, sitting above (2) a black zigzag wave line of about seven rounded peaks and troughs, stroked at roughly 1 percent of slide height, spanning the width of the mark, above (3) the wordmark 'FYRE' in black geometric sans-serif capitals, widely letter-spaced, medium-bold weight. Below the lockup, centred near the bottom, is the URL 'FYREAPP.COM' in thin, widely spaced capitals in orange #FC8002. No photographs, charts or other graphics.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Minimal monochrome look: pure white background, black logo mark and wordmark, single orange accent for the URL. Wordmark is a geometric sans-serif (close to Visby CF Medium) in capitals with wide letter-spacing, about 4.5 percent of slide height. URL is Visby CF Thin in capitals, about 1.6 percent of slide height, orange #FC8002. Line and shapes are flat with no shadows, gradients or corner radii beyond the rounded zigzag ends.
